# Appendix: every free-text answer, by persona

## Must-have levers (165)

- B01-P01: Model operating-expense structures inside the comparison (base year / expense stop, growth rate, USF-to-RSF load factor) so mixed gross and NNN proposals compare like for like.
- B01-P02: Verified building-spec facts (clear height, docks, trailer stalls, power) with source document and last-verified date, joined to parcel IDs across logistics markets.
- B01-P03: Maricopa County owner-of-record plus a parent/affiliate link that classifies the owner as health system, REIT or private.
- B01-P04: Cited drive-thru permissibility by zoning district for my four metros, with ordinance section and code version date.
- B01-P05: An amendment-aware lease-fact tool with page-level citations and a published critical-date accuracy number.
- B01-P06: Building-level debt facts (lender, balance, maturity, servicing status) covering balance-sheet and debt-fund loans, joined to the building ID.
- B01-P07: A power-native quote normaliser ($/kW all-in vs metered, MW ramp schedule) plus utility/moratorium facts by county.
- B01-P08: A contribute-to-earn lease comp exchange covering Boise and Spokane.
- B01-P09: Building-level loan and servicing facts from a licensed or public source, each with the same per-fact AI-use rights, plus an indemnity clause.
- B01-P10: A published reconciliation to ARGUS/VTS NER conventions with selectable methods (amortised TI/LC, discount rate, day-count).
- B01-P11: Lease expiration estimates by building with a confidence score, joined to parcel IDs in Phoenix and Las Vegas.
- B01-P12: A conflict-check over structured, cited exclusives and prohibited uses (with amendment precedence) for a center's leases and REAs.
- B01-P13: A multifamily mode: monthly rent per unit, concessions in weeks/months, batch evaluation across a rent roll, and an auditable rationale record.
- B01-P14: Storage supply-pipeline alerts from permits and planning agendas within 3-5 miles of each facility.
- B01-P15: An admin-fee (and fee-on-fee) input plus a batch CAM reconciliation that returns tenant-ready statements for every tenant.
- B01-P16: Expansion signals (new licences, permits, job postings) for local and regional F&B and fitness operators, with contacts and CRM write-back.
- B01-P17: A building ID minting rule (from a parcel or an address) with suite normalisation that cannot merge distinct suites, and VTS/Salesforce write-back.
- B02-P01: Sales comps for DFW/Houston multifamily with cap rate, its basis (T-3/T-12/pro forma) and source for every row.
- B02-P02: A cited lease-abstract record per NNN listing: guarantor entity, remaining term, bump schedule, each with the OM page.
- B02-P03: An abstract-lease-bundle tool that returns the current-state lease with each field citing document and page and the amendment that last changed it.
- B02-P04: A parse-USALI-P&L tool that turns an operating statement PDF into the hotel_metrics inputs with each line cited to its page.
- B02-P05: Deed owner LLC to parent to sponsor chain for NYC with recorded-document citations and linked CMBS loan IDs.
- B02-P06: Ohio, Indiana and Kentucky coverage with an asset-type filter and recorded mortgage lender/maturity per facility.
- B02-P07: Per-parcel zoning/ETJ, floodplain and utility-district facts with ordinance or case-number citations.
- B02-P08: A clause-level lease abstraction tool with healthcare-specific clause types (ROFR, radius, relocation, ground lease consent) cited to page.
- B02-P09: A reconcile tool that takes a rent roll and lease terms and returns each disagreement with the lease page cited.
- B02-P10: A building-level canonical ID with crosswalks to county APN and third-party IDs, spec values with source per field, and quarterly change history delivered in bulk.
- B02-P11: Multi-program sizing in one call with partial-IO periods and the full amortization schedule returned.
- B02-P12: A MW-based data-center schema and pipeline: campus, planned/UC/delivered MW, substation and energization date, each cited to the filing and date.
- B02-P13: A verify_claim tool that returns supported, contradicted or unverifiable for a claim against supplied documents, with citations.
- B02-P14: Tenant-to-parent entity mapping with credit events and store closures by address, linked to cited co-tenancy clauses.
- B02-P15: A permit-to-parcel matcher for Boise, Salt Lake City and Reno returning projects with parcel ID, units or SF, status history and permit links.
- B02-P16: AI-licensed lease comps for Bay Area and LA office with starting rent, free rent, TI, NER and a verified-vs-reported flag, feeding the NER calculator.
- B02-P17: Insurance premium per unit distributions by submarket and vintage with sample size and sources, plus Florida millage and tax-reset history.
- B03-P01: A lease-abstract-to-underwriting pipeline: reconcile a rent roll against lease facts with page citations and emit an ARGUS-importable file.
- B03-P02: A multifamily underwriting bundle: T-12 chart-of-accounts mapper plus a waterfall that returns auditable LP/GP cash flows.

- B03-P15: Expose lease ingest as a free-tier tool that returns a rent schedule, term, options and repair/CAM obligations with page citations from a scanned PDF, flagging low-confidence OCR for human review.
- B03-P16: Signed fact packages: a hash-chained, signed bundle of cited lease facts with a verifier that re-checks each quote against the data-room PDF.
- B03-P17: Parcel-matched environmental-listing and zoning-approval facts, each with its source record, date and match confidence.

## Marketing: unclear or unconvincing (513)

- B01-P01: Denver is listed among loaded registry metros (README.md, site-index.txt Registry) but the registry is parcels/owners only and 'no sales prices, tenants, debt, zoning, or building specs beyond area and year built' (README.md) - nothing in it helps a market survey
- B01-P01: site-index.txt says 'there is no public endpoint yet' (Connect section) and README.md says 'no hosted public endpoint', while the Pricing section and llms.txt present https://api.factlot.com/mcp as a live free tier; a reader cannot tell which is current (I could not test api.factlot.com, only the local build)
- B01-P01: Pro and Team (site-index.txt Pricing) buy higher rate limits and 'hosted registry lookups as each region opens (none is open yet)': today a paid plan buys no data
- B01-P02: 'Canonical IDs for buildings' (README.md) - there is no tool that mints a building ID from a parcel in the tool list
- B01-P02: Registry coverage numbers differ between README.md (708,538 + 1,008,126 parcels) and site-index.txt (1,953,805) and none of it includes building specs
- B01-P02: site-index.txt says 'there is no public endpoint yet' (Connect section) and README.md says 'no hosted public endpoint', while the Pricing section and llms.txt present https://api.factlot.com/mcp as a live free tier; a reader cannot tell which is current (I could not test api.factlot.com, only the local build)
- B01-P03: Maricopa is not among the loaded counties and the README says 'Maricopa ... wait on a licence decision, a form or a purchase' - a reader has to find that sentence in the middle of a paragraph
- B01-P03: Nothing says whether 'owner type' (health system vs REIT vs LLC) is derivable
- B01-P03: Pro and Team (site-index.txt Pricing) buy higher rate limits and 'hosted registry lookups as each region opens (none is open yet)': today a paid plan buys no data

- B01-P04: Owner 'contact' is not offered; the Terms forbid using it to 'contact private individuals' (site-terms.txt)
- B01-P05: No statement on SOC 2 or enterprise terms; the Terms page is 'as is' with liability capped at three months' fees (site-terms.txt)
- B01-P05: Amendment-aware abstraction is described (site-index.txt Rules and Ingest) but not exposed as a tool in the build I called
- B01-P05: site-index.txt says 'there is no public endpoint yet' (Connect section) and README.md says 'no hosted public endpoint', while the Pricing section and llms.txt present https://api.factlot.com/mcp as a live free tier; a reader cannot tell which is current (I could not test api.factlot.com, only the local build)

- B01-P07: Nothing in site-index.txt, README.md or the plugin README mentions power, MW, $/kW or colocation
- B01-P07: Hotel metrics is the only non-lease asset-type calculator advertised; data centers are not mentioned
- B01-P08: No mention of a comp contribution / give-to-get scheme; the business-model page is linked but not in my materials
- B01-P08: site-index.txt says 'there is no public endpoint yet' (Connect section) and README.md says 'no hosted public endpoint', while the Pricing section and llms.txt present https://api.factlot.com/mcp as a live free tier; a reader cannot tell which is current (I could only test the local build)

- B03-P01: The home headline numbers (1,953,805 parcels, 405 counties, 21 states) read as available product, but the Pricing section says hosted registry lookups are 'none open yet' and the only downloadable data is the DC sample (llms.txt); a REIT agent cannot tell what it can actually query for Inland Empire or DFW today.
- B03-P01: Nothing says whether a registry fact includes industrial attributes such as clear height or dock count; the DC parcel I pulled has area, value and use code only.
- B03-P01: Enterprise terms such as 'no-training terms' and private deployment are offered by email, but SOC 2 / data residency / audit-log questions that a REIT procurement asks are not addressed in the materials I read.
- B03-P02: Nothing in the materials mentions multifamily underwriting inputs: T-12 normalisation, loss-to-lease, concession burn-off, LP waterfall. A multifamily syndicator cannot tell if any of that is planned (docs/roadmap.md was not in the materials I was given).
- B03-P02: 'Current agency spreads' are not offered; the debt-sizing tool takes a rate I must source myself (inferred from the tool schema).
- B03-P02: The unit of the paid plans is requests per minute, not per OM or per deal, so it does not map to my per-OM budgeting.
- B03-P03: The coverage paragraph on the home page lists named large metros and whole states; no GA, SC or NC county is named except Charlotte, so I cannot tell if Greenville SC is covered, and hosted registry is 'none open yet' anyway.
- B03-P03: No storage-specific mention anywhere in the materials: 'Commercial, industrial, and multifamily parcels only' (site-index.txt) suggests self-storage may or may not be a covered category (not tested outside DC).
- B03-P03: Per-call pricing is not offered; plans are per-minute rate limits (Pro $49, Team $599), which does not match 'a few dollars per property report'.
- B03-P04: 'Parcel to owner of record to registered entity' sounds like it reaches sponsors; in the product the chain stops at the registered entity (a name and a mailing address), not at the sponsor behind it (see calls). The page never says it will resolve SPE to sponsor.
- B03-P04: Home copy lists loaded counties; Chicago/Cook, Hennepin, Milwaukee, St. Louis are named or implied only partly (Minneapolis and Wisconsin are named; Chicago and St. Louis are not in the paragraph), and hosted registry is 'none open yet'.
- B03-P04: No account of how an 'inferred' link with 0.9 confidence is set, beyond the basis string in the output.
- B03-P05: 'Parcel to owner of record to registered entity' might reach tenant entities, but the registry is about owners of commercial parcels, not tenants; the materials never say tenants are out of scope for the registry, only for 'the data' in llms.txt.
- B03-P05: Nothing about NPI, CMS or state affiliation sources; my trust requirement (source links to NPI/CMS/state records) is not mentioned anywhere in the registry description on the home page (I did not see the full source catalog).
- B03-P05: Home copy offers no healthcare example at all.
- B03-P06: Home page says the rules packs 'turn jurisdiction law into code' but only one pack exists (Texas protest deadline, verified 2026-11-02) and it is property-tax only; there is no signal of ordinance or moratorium tracking.
- B03-P06: Tool inputs are named area_sf and psf (tool list), so a MW or $/kW/month lease needs a unit relabelling hack; the materials do not say this is supported.
- B03-P07: The CAM skill does not mention admin/management fee on top of pool, or fee-then-cap ordering; my own hardest problem ('admin fee and caps compound') is not addressed in the materials.
- B03-P07: No co-tenancy cascade tool is listed; the term appears only as a predicate name in the schema vocabulary (see calls), which a reader could mistake for a capability.
- B03-P07: Ingest is a prototype and there is no demo input/output for a lease with amendments in the materials I was given.
- B03-P08: The page does not say the hotel tool takes period totals and does not normalise a raw P&L; 'Hotel P&L normalizer' is on my missing list and this is not it (see calls).
- B03-P08: No insurance, flood zone, elevation or storm-surge claim anywhere; the registry description is commercial/industrial/multifamily parcels only, which excludes hotels by name (site-index.txt), so I cannot tell if a 160-key hotel parcel is even in scope.
- B03-P08: Florida is not named in the loaded-registry list on the home page.
- B03-P09: The registry's 'registered entity' idea sounds like it could resolve a franchisee guarantor, but the materials never say it links an operating tenant to its franchisor or lists its unit count.
- B03-P09: Coverage is by region and none of it is hosted yet ('none open yet'), so nationwide screening is not possible from the materials.
- B03-P09: No tool for date arithmetic such as the 45-day identification window; it is not claimed anywhere.
- B03-P10: The only quote-on-page verification is attributed to 'Ingest (a prototype)'. It is not in the tool list, so a prospect cannot try it; fact_validate (the tool they can call) does not check quotes (see calls).
- B03-P10: 'A validator for cited facts' (home, 'For AI agents') and 'a validated, cited fact' (llms.txt) can be read as source verification; the tool description says only 'conforms to the FactLot model'.
- B03-P10: No audit-log, SOC 2 or bar-ethics language in the materials; the immutable audit trail I need is not described.
- B03-P11: Nothing in the materials mentions estoppels, SNDAs, e-sign or Yardi at all, so the connection to my job is only by inference.
- B03-P11: Home says IDs for 'buildings, spaces, leases' exist but the building ID is only a format; there is no tool that creates a building URN from an address (id tool list), so the join to Yardi is left to me.
- B03-P11: No example reconciling two parties' differing figures, which is the dispute case.
- B03-P12: 'Every fact points at the document it came from' sounds like instrument-level citation; in the product it points at the dataset file (see calls), not a recorded instrument or image. The home page says 'the public file it was read from', which is accurate, but my dealbreaker 'no document images behind facts' still applies.
- B03-P12: Texas is described as loaded, but I could only test DC; the hosted service does not serve registry tools, so a Texas lawyer cannot try it without downloading a Texas database, and only the DC sample is offered for download.
- B03-P12: Nothing on legal descriptions, easements, MUD/PID overlays or survey data.
- B03-P13: 'Rules packs turn jurisdiction law into code' reads broad; the only pack is the Texas protest deadline. The roadmap for zoning or municipal-code packs is not in the materials.
- B03-P13: Coverage claims for the registry are by county list; my need is 3,000+ jurisdictions' zoning, and nothing indicates municipal-code coverage.
- B03-P13: The 'Coverage gaps' principle: the product says a gap is a gap, but see calls for how misses look.
- B03-P14: Nothing on stress scenarios: 'stress' appears nowhere in the materials, so I cannot tell if a sensitivity run is one call or ten.
- B03-P14: No mention of rent control (AB 1482 or local), wildfire or insurance, or rent-roll-to-bank tie-out, all of which are in my record.
- B03-P14: Sponsor entity resolution is implied by 'registered entity', but California is not named in the loaded-registry list except Los Angeles and San Francisco.
- B03-P15: Ingest 'turns leases and amendments into cited facts' but is labelled a prototype and is not callable in the tool list, so a solo attorney cannot try it.
- B03-P15: No mention of OCR quality or phone scans anywhere; the SourceRef schema has an `ocr` flag, but nothing says an OCR step exists.
- B03-P15: The page is built around institutional use; nothing says whether a one-person firm can run the local server without a developer (Node 22.6+ is required).
- B03-P16: Nothing mentions signing or attestation, so 'portable and verifiable' is by inference: the validator checks structure, not truth (see calls).
- B03-P16: Ingest is called a prototype and is not in the tool list; no example shows a lease chain with misfiled amendments being assembled.
- B03-P16: No mention of data-room integration (Datasite, Q&A) or per-bidder access control.
- B03-P17: The home page lists Pennsylvania entity registries but does not list New Jersey among loaded states; for my Elizabeth NJ example I cannot tell if parcels or entities exist.
- B03-P17: 'Building-centric' is my dealbreaker: the registry record is parcel-based and gives land area, but nothing in the materials says land-heavy uses (yards) are classified or searchable as such.
- B03-P17: No environmental or stormwater source appears in the materials.

- B09-P01: No statement of whether registry data may be used in rating or underwriting decisions: site-terms.txt is short and I found nothing on rating use (a stated dealbreaker for me)
- B09-P01: No bulk/batch endpoint is mentioned; the REST API is one lookup per call
- B09-P02: Nothing on insurance, flood, wind or tax data; the home page talks about leases and owners, not physical risk or property tax

- B09-P02: No methodology document set aimed at model validation (SR 11-7); the steps are in each response but I saw no validation package
- B09-P03: Registry hosted lookups 'as each region opens (none is open yet)' appears on every paid tier (site-index.txt), so there is nothing I can call today at api.factlot.com for a property profile
- B09-P03: Home page never says what a parcel record contains (area? stories? roof?); I had to call it to learn it has area, year built and value only
- B09-P03: Nothing about storage, hail, or construction

- B09-P04: No attestation or lineage-export endpoint, which is the one thing I would pay for

## Marketing: contradicted by the product (8)

- B01-P09: site-index.txt Principles: 'Everything cited. Uncited facts are rejected.' - fact_validate returned valid:true for a debt.special_servicing = true fact with method 'inferred' and sources [] (call 2)
- B04-P17: site-index.txt says a rules pack stays draft 'until a named person has checked it against the official text'; the verification field the rule returned (call 4) names 'FactLot maintainers' as verifier, a group, not a named person. Minor, but the audience for a rule I would cite to a client is exactly the one that asks who verified it.
- B06-P11: llms.txt: 'deterministic FactLot IDs for parcels, legal entities, buildings, suites, leases, and documents'. GET /v1/tools shows id_parcel, id_entity, id_space, id_document, id_parse only; id_parse accepted a lease and a building URN I typed myself (id_parse and id_space calls), so a building or lease ID is not deterministic from any input the product provides
- B07-P02: Tool description says address search normalizes 'Street' = 'St', 'Suite' = 'Ste'; in my calls 'Avenue' normalized to Ave but 'Northwest' did not match NW and a query with a suite appended returned nothing (calls 2, 3). That is narrower than a reader assumes, not a false claim.
- B07-P05: The home page says a value is kept only if its quote supports it, but the public fact_validate tool accepted a lease expiration value 2033-06-30 whose quote read 'June 30, 2031' as valid (call 1). The tool description only claims shape-checking, and the check is attributed to Ingest, which is not in the catalog, so this is a gap in what I can reach rather than a lie.
- B08-P14: 'Uncited facts are rejected' (site-index.txt Principles): fact_validate accepted a fact with method 'inferred' and sources [] (call 2). The validator's own message exempts computed/inferred facts from the citation requirement.
- B09-P01: Marketing line 'Stable FactLot IDs for parcels, legal entities, buildings, spaces ... computable offline' (site-index.txt): the building ID is not computable offline per the fetched spec:factlot-id; id_space needs an already-minted building URN (call: fetch spec:factlot-id)
- B09-P06: site-index.txt: IDs for 'buildings ... computable offline' - the fetched ID spec says building IDs are minted tokens (call: fetch spec:factlot-id); I did not find a building resolver in /v1/tools

## Marketing: headline for my role (165)

- B01-P01: Compare 5 landlord proposals on one discounted $/SF/yr basis, with every step and a formula ID you can show the client.
- B01-P02: Compare multi-market lease offers on one discounted $/SF/yr basis and key every site to a stable parcel ID.
- B01-P03: Turn an LLC owner of record into a cited registry entity and compare medical TI packages on net effective cost.
- B01-P04: Cited owner-of-record and entity lookups for pad parcels, plus percentage-rent math for the deal.
- B01-P05: Run the same auditable renew-vs-relocate NPV on every expiring lease, self-hosted inside the client's network.
- B01-P06: Rank direct vs sublease options on one discounted cost basis with every step shown, for client memos.
- B01-P07: (none for data centers) - a generic discounted-cost calculator you can repurpose with a kW 'area'.
- B01-P08: Free: check any lease counter-offer's net effective rent and effective cost in seconds, with the steps to show your client.
- B01-P09: Every registry fact says what its source is, when it was read and whether AI may use it.
- B01-P10: Re-run every proposal and counter on the same discounted NER, with a formula ID and steps your approval memo can cite.
- B01-P11: Price a 220,000 SF proposal against alternatives on discounted net effective rent in one call.
- B01-P12: Run each tenant's CAM true-up and percentage-rent calc deterministically, with caps and gross-up shown line by line.
- B01-P13: (limited) Turn a concession like 'one month free' into an effective monthly rent you can log.
- B01-P14: (none) - only the ancillary-space lease math applies.
- B01-P15: Run each tenant's CAM true-up deterministically, self-hosted, with steps you can send to a tenant who disputes the bill.
- B01-P16: Price three ground-floor bay proposals on a like-for-like discounted NER and compute percentage rent exactly.
- B01-P17: One stable, offline-computable ID per suite that every system can use to refer to the same space.
- B02-P01: Size the new loan and price the seller's payoff alternative on one page, with every step and formula ID you can paste into the BOV.
- B02-P02: Turn an OM's rent bumps into a cited rent schedule, remaining-term WALT and a hold-period IRR in three tool calls, free.
- B02-P03: Rent schedules, net effective rent and WALT for a 41-tenant park that reproduce to the cent, with the discounting convention printed in the steps.
- B02-P04: Feed in a hotel's annual P&L lines and get the USALI ladder back (occupancy, ADR, RevPAR, TRevPAR, GOP, GOPPAR, EBITDA, reserve, NOI), every step shown.
- B02-P05: Each owner of record resolves to a registered entity with the source file, record locator and licence on every fact, so you can show it to a client.
- B02-P06: Owner of record to registered LLC for commercial parcels, each fact cited to the public file it came from.
- B02-P07: A deterministic parcel ID you can compute offline for any Texas county, plus a verified protest-deadline date for land owners.
- B02-P08: Store each clause as a fact with the page and verbatim quote attached, and compute WALT across the suites.
- B02-P09: Recompute what each lease says the rent should be today, so a rent roll that disagrees stands out before it reaches the OM.
- B02-P10: One deterministic key per parcel and suite that every system can compute offline, with the source and licence on each spec value.
- B02-P11: Size a refi under LTV, DSCR and debt yield in one call and get the binding constraint with every step, free.
- B02-P12: Honest answer: no true headline for data centers; the nearest true claim is exact lease arithmetic with an audit trail, but in SF units.
- B02-P13: Every calculation reproduces identically from the same inputs over REST and MCP, with a versioned formula ID and its steps, so an audit committee can re-run a GP's IRR.
- B02-P14: For a tenant that is a registered entity, get its registry record with the source file and an ID you can join to your own tenant table.
- B02-P15: A deterministic parcel ID you can compute for any county from its printed APN, so permit and assessor records can be joined on one key.
- B02-P16: Net effective rent after free rent, TI and commissions, three ways (effective, straight-line, discounted), every step shown and every page example reproducible.
- B02-P17: Honest answer: nothing for insurance or climate; the nearest true claim is that any figure you do have can be stored as a cited fact and run through exact loan math.
- B03-P01: Deterministic rent-schedule, net-effective-rent and lease-proposal ranking math with every step and a versioned formula ID, free to self-host so lease data never leaves your tenant.
- B03-P02: Size agency vs bridge debt on the same NOI in two calls and see which of LTV, DSCR or debt yield binds, with the formula shown.
- B03-P03: Free, auditable debt-sizing, IRR and NPV for the storage pro forma you already built; owner-of-record lookups where the public-record registry is loaded.
- B03-P04: Cited, AI-use-cleared owner-of-record and entity lookups from public records, with inferred links labelled and scored, plus an audited re-leasing economics calculator.
- B03-P05: Deterministic ground-lease and MOB rent schedules and WALT with every step shown, free to run in your own tenancy.
- B03-P06: Not for this role yet: the only usable pieces are the audited rent math (with units relabelled) and offline parcel IDs for a site in a county the registry does not cover.
- B03-P07: A CAM and percentage-rent reconciliation that shows every step, handles gross-up, exclusions and three kinds of cap, and returns a balance you can bill or credit.
- B03-P08: A USALI-style hotel profit ladder (RevPAR to NOI after FF&E reserve) with every line's formula shown, so an insurance shock flows straight to NOI.
- B03-P09: Audited rent-schedule and IRR math for a net lease with fixed bumps, so a cap rate becomes a yield with the steps shown.
- B03-P10: An open fact format where every extracted term carries document hash, page, span, quote and a supersedes link, self-hostable so client documents never leave your tenancy.
- B03-P11: Recompute a disputed CAM reconciliation under each reading of the cap clause and see the exact dollar difference, with every step.
- B03-P12: Cited current owner-of-record and registered-entity lookups from county appraisal rolls and state registries, with the licence and AI-use status of each source - a starting point, not a title search.
- B03-P13: Nothing for zoning yet; the Texas rules pack shows the model (statute links, version, verifier and date, explicit 'not covered' list) a zoning pack would follow.
- B03-P14: Size a bridge or take-out loan under LTV, DSCR and debt yield in one deterministic call, with every constraint's loan amount shown, so a stressed-rent run is reproducible.
- B03-P15: Check any lease's stated rent table against a deterministic schedule, free, with the formula and warnings shown and a disclaimer your client can read.
- B03-P16: An open, self-hostable format for lease facts that carries page, quote, hash, supersession and AI-use rights, which buyers' agents can read without a proprietary tool.
- B03-P17: Cited parcel facts (land area, assessed value, owner of record) and the registered entity behind an LLC, with inferred links labelled and scored, plus per-acre yard rent math.
- B04-P01: Every lease-economics number comes back with its steps and a versioned formula ID, so the human can re-run it and the IC can see how it was made.
- B04-P02: CAM and cap math that returns the same answer every time, with every step and the formula ID, so the paralegal does not re-do it in Excel.
- B04-P03: Size the loan under LTV, DSCR and debt yield in one call and see which constraint binds, with the arithmetic in the steps.
- B04-P04: The CAM arithmetic (pro rata, base year, gross-up, caps) is done by a deterministic tool, not by the language model that mis-prorated my tenant.
- B04-P05: Every owner and entity fact comes with its source file, record number and a stated confidence, so the board can see the evidence chain.
- B04-P06: Every value carries the document ID, page and quote it came from, so a lender-facing estoppel field can be traced back to the amendment text.
- B04-P07: Join Brooklyn and Queens parcels, owners and assessments by one canonical BBL-based ID, with each fact linked to the public record it came from.
- B04-P08: One deterministic ID per parcel, suite and document, so a seller's spreadsheet, the county parcel and our system can be joined, with per-field source, verifier and timestamp.
- B04-P09: I cannot write one from the materials: no true claim touches storage supply or permits.
- B04-P10: ASC 842 remeasurement as a deterministic calculation with the paragraph references and every step shown, so the auditor can recompute it.
- B04-P11: USALI-style hotel metrics (RevPAR, GOPPAR, EBITDA, NOI) computed deterministically, with each step shown, so budget packages can be compared on one basis.
- B04-P12: Reproducible rent math with every step shown, and parcel facts that cite the public record they came from, so each arbitration input has a source and a date.

## Problems (413)

- B01-P01: Opex is a flat $/SF/yr for the whole term and there is no base-year/expense-stop or opex growth input, so 'base year 2027 gross' and 'NNN + $14.50' cannot be compared fairly over 7 years (call 3)
- B01-P01: Tenant-side 'additional costs' is the only place to put pass-throughs; USF vs RSF proposals still need the agent to convert areas itself (call 3)
- B01-P01: Empty registry result for Denver is indistinguishable from 'not found' (call 6)
- B01-P02: Validator accepts a fact whose citation lacks page and quote, and has no 'verified' flag or last-verified date beyond as_of, so it cannot enforce my 'spec data verified' criterion (calls 6, 8)
- B01-P02: Cross-market ranking is a pure lease-cost metric; no way to include drive-time, labor or spec differences (call 1)
- B01-P02: Empty/null registry answers do not say the region is not loaded (call 4)
- B01-P03: No concept of a parent/ultimate owner, so 'health system vs REIT vs LLC' still needs a person (call 3)
- B01-P03: Name search is substring only and returns unrelated 'HOSPITALITY' entities (call 1)
- B01-P03: Medical buildout is a single lump sum in a generic field (call 6)
- B01-P04: No path from an LLC owner to a decision-maker (call 5)
- B01-P04: A parcel with no owner fact looks identical to a gap in the data (call 3)
- B01-P04: The `search` knowledge tool returned a schema doc for a zoning query rather than saying zoning is out of scope (call 1)
- B01-P05: One site per call: a 38-site strategy needs 38 calls and my own aggregation (call 1)
- B01-P05: Relocation costs (TI, FF&E, moving) are inputs I must source; no benchmarks (record, call 1)
- B01-P05: The only sensitivity returned is the pair of break-even starting rents; no discount-rate or horizon scan, so a portfolio memo needs extra calls (call 1)
- B01-P06: Ranking uses a level annual $/SF metric over each proposal's own term; proposals with different terms are ranked with no warning that term length and re-leasing risk are not priced (call 1)
- B01-P06: Nothing in the registry answers 'can this landlord fund $250/SF of TI' (calls 2, 3)
- B01-P06: Generic tenant_upfront_costs is the only slot for lab fit-out overage (call 1)
- B01-P07: Labels read 'USD/sf/yr' and 'effective_occupancy_cost_psf' for what is $/kW/yr; a client deliverable would need relabelling (call 1)
- B01-P07: No ramp (capacity delivered in tranches) in the compare tool; NPV had to be built by hand (call 4)
- B01-P07: No warning that kW is being treated as square feet (call 1)
- B01-P08: No comps or give-to-get exchange, so FMV is still the broker's gut (call 5)
- B01-P08: No Idaho/Washington registry (calls 3-4)
- B01-P09: An uncited 'inferred' distress fact validates, which is the 'hallucinated distress signal' risk I flagged as a dealbreaker (call 2)
- B01-P09: No debt data to anchor any distress claim (call 1)
- B01-P09: Terms offer no indemnity (site-terms.txt)
- B01-P09: Ranking uses a level annual $/SF metric over each proposal's own term; proposals with different terms are ranked with no warning that term length and re-leasing risk are not priced (call 4)
- B01-P10: Cannot confirm ARGUS-matching 'within rounding' - no ARGUS in this review, and the materials do not claim it
- B01-P10: Leasing commissions are a lump sum I must compute (call 1)
- B01-P10: TI and LC treated as paid at commencement; amortised-TI/LC methodologies (my firm's convention) are not options (tool input schema: TI is 'paid once at commencement')
- B01-P11: Shortest term ranks first with no pricing of vacancy/re-leasing risk and no warning (call 1)
- B01-P11: No prospecting or comps data of any kind (call 4)
- B01-P12: The central task - an exclusive conflict check before an LOI - has no tool (call 5)
- B01-P12: The CAM input list (GET /v1/tools) has no admin-fee field, so a lease's administrative fee has to be computed outside the tool and added as an expense line
- B01-P12: One tenant per call; 25 inline leases means 25 calls and my own roll-up (call 1)
- B01-P13: Everything is $/SF/yr; an apartment's rent has to be converted by hand and the output reads 'USD/sf/yr' (call 1)
- B01-P13: No batch endpoint: 180 renewals means 180 calls (tool list: single-lease inputs only)
- B01-P13: Partial-month free rent not supported (call 3)
- B01-P14: No storage content at all (calls 1-2)
- B01-P14: The only calls that apply are for commercial tenants in my buildings, a minor part of the job (call 3)
- B01-P15: No admin-fee parameter, a standard NNN-lease term: the agent must compute it outside the tool, reintroducing in-context math (calls 1, 8)
- B01-P15: Validator does not check that superseded facts exist (call 6)
- B01-P15: One tenant per call; 140 reconciliations means 140 calls (tool list)
- B01-P16: Nothing on operator viability (calls 3-4)
- B01-P16: Ranking across different-sized bays gives a rate metric, not a merchandising decision (call 1)
- B01-P17: Numeric suite normalisation can merge distinct suites: 'Suite 1-20' = 'Suite 120', 'Suite 1-2' = 'Suite 12' (call 3)
- B01-P17: Penthouse/PH and 'Ground floor'/'Lower Level' do not alias; a caller needs its own mapping (call 2)
- B01-P17: There is no way to mint a building ID from my data, so id_space cannot be used end to end (calls 6-7)
- B02-P01: calc_debt_sizing has no input for an existing loan (balance, maturity, supplemental eligibility), so 'assume vs new debt' is only half-supported (call 2).
- B02-P01: Registry parcel facts are tax-roll facts (owner, appraised value, areas); no sale price, loan or cap-rate fact exists (call 7).
- B02-P01: A county outside the loaded set returns null with no explanation (call 6).
- B02-P02: No tool covers listings, guarantor, credit, or 1031 deadlines (call 1).
- B02-P02: fact_validate accepted a fact citing an invented sha256 document ID, so 'valid' does not mean the OM says it (call 6).
- B02-P02: Cap rate is not a calculator input: I had to derive 6.5% (208,000/3.2M) myself.
- B02-P03: fact_validate passes impossible dates (valid_from after valid_to) (call 6).
- B02-P03: No tool resolves which amendment wins; validity is shape-only (call 5).
- B02-P03: The NER convention (beginning-of-month discounting) differs from some shops' month-end convention; it is disclosed in the step formula, but a comp set mixing conventions would not reconcile (call 2).
- B02-P04: NOI in this tool is EBITDA less an FF&E reserve; a lender 'NOI' may be defined before reserve, so the label needs checking per deal (call 1 step formula states it).
- B02-P04: The fact model has no field to flag that a price per key is a portfolio allocation; it accepted the comp as valid with free-form predicates (call 5).
- B02-P04: My PIP IRR was negative on the numbers I chose; that is my input, not the tool (call 4).
- B02-P05: The ownership link is inferred from an exact name match at 0.9 confidence; the DC owner mailing-address string for 1801 K reads 'EAST TOWER 26F MIRAE ASSET CENTER1 26EUL, SEOUL 00000 NORTH KOREA' as returned, which is a visibly odd country value that a client would question (call 1).
- B02-P05: 1050 Connecticut Ave NW (967,088 SF) returned no owner fact and no ownership link at all (call 4); disclosed on the home page ('some public files omit owner names') but it leaves a gap exactly on the big assets.
- B02-P05: The entity record has no parent, officer or sponsor data (call 3).
- B02-P05: Registry values use raw codes (property category F1, local use code) without decoded labels (calls 1, 4).
- B02-P06: Entity names are the only handle for asset type: storage owners can only be found by luck of naming (calls 1, 2).
- B02-P06: No purchase date, no loan, no person contact, no zoning in registry facts: the parcel facts are owner, mailing address, values, areas, year built, use code (call 5).
- B02-P06: About 30% of the parcels in my DC sample had no owner of record (see scan row); disclosed, but it caps what a prospecting list can contain.
- B02-P06: The entity mailing address is a street address from the business registry; it is not a verified, DNC-checked contact, so I could not use it as one.
- B02-P07: No zoning, entitlement or floodplain predicates exist in the materials or tool list; the fact-model summary fetched from the server defines generic fact fields (subject, predicate, value, provenance, rights, supersedes) and no land-use predicates (call 8).
- B02-P07: I did not independently verify the statute reading beyond the tool's cited text (call 4).
- B02-P07: Registry cannot answer for Texas on this server (call 7).
- B02-P08: fact_validate accepted value 90 days against a quote that says thirty (30) days; it checks shape, not content (call 4).
- B02-P08: There is no ROFR/ground-lease predicate vocabulary; I used a free-form 'lease.rofr' (call 3).
- B02-P08: The registry search for medical office returned two entities (call 6) with no property-use filter; one name contains a person's name, which is an LLC legal name, not a natural-person record.
- B02-P09: No reconcile tool: the discrepancy flag is manual (call 1).
- B02-P09: id_document gives a hash, not a page-level citation; a buyer-question log would still need a page/paragraph scheme of its own (calls 4-6).
- B02-P09: Nothing for NDA or data-room actions.
- B02-P10: No building-level ID tool, only parcel and suite (call 1).
- B02-P10: The only crosswalk on the parcel is the county key (dc_ssl); no CoStar or other vendor ID appears (call 2).
- B02-P10: Local use codes and property category are raw ('047', 'F1') with no decoder in the output (call 2).
- B02-P11: No amortization schedule in the response, though my single wish asks for one (calls 2-5 returned value, steps, warnings, provenance only).
- B02-P11: One program per call: comparing four programs took four calls.
- B02-P11: No partial-IO structure; amortization_years=0 means pure IO (call 5, inputs).
- B02-P11: DSCR was the binding constraint in all four of my invented programs; I did not test cases where debt yield or LTV binds beyond the README example.
- B02-P12: The workaround is not a supported use: fields and labels still say SF ('rent_psf_annual', effective_rent_psf 1994.715) so a reader of the output could misread units (calls 4, 5).
- B02-P12: Searches for Equinix, Digital Realty and 'data center' in the DC sample returned no entities (calls 1-3).
- B02-P12: The Texas deadline rule exists but nothing for the other states I cover (call 6).
- B02-P13: IRR 'steps' has two entries; it shows the method but not a period-by-period table, so the audit trail is thinner than for the rent tools (call 1).
- B02-P13: fact_validate accepted a GP-claimed 60,000 SF with a deck-page citation while the registry says 53,236 SF; it cannot compare claim to source, and nothing in the product does (calls 5, 6).
- B02-P13: Registry facts here come from one DC tax roll; my test of 'verification of GP-claimed facts' could only be done by hand.
- B02-P14: Brand-name coverage depends on whether the company happens to be registered in the registry's jurisdiction: 'Kroger' returned nothing and 'Aldi' returned one DC foreign registration (calls 1, 2).
- B02-P14: The entity record has no parent company or brand field (call 3).
- B02-P14: fact_validate accepts a credit event with an example URL and a made-up document hash; there is no credit-event feed behind it (call 8).
- B02-P15: ID normalization drops separators: APNs '12-34' and '1-234' both returned fl:parcel:us-32031:1234 (calls 11, 12), so distinct parcels could collide in a county that prints separators positionally; I did not find a real county example.
- B02-P15: Registry lookups for Ada County and 'Boise' addresses returned nothing, with no 'not loaded' message (calls 4, 5).
- B02-P15: No permit, unit-count or rent tools.
- B02-P16: The ranking flips between straight-line and discounted NER (call 2), so a comp set needs one stated convention; the tool states it but the home page does not recommend one.
- B02-P16: The discounting timing (rent at the start of each month, annual rate / 12) is a choice; I replicated it but did not compare with a third-party convention.
- B02-P16: No existing-loan input for the distress screen (call 4).
- B02-P17: No insurance, millage or climate tool exists; the search tool returns tool entries unrelated to the query (calls 1, 2).
- B02-P17: fact_validate has no field for sample size or distribution; an insurance benchmark fact would carry that only inside its value (call 6).
- B02-P17: The reassessment and insurance adjustments were my own arithmetic; there is no tax-reassessment calculator (call 5).
- B03-P01: No downtime / speculative-leasing or renewal-probability modeling: compare_proposals ranks on each deal's own commencement (call 3), so a blended mark-to-market on rollover is still my work.
- B03-P01: Discounting is start-of-month; a lender or ARGUS-aligned team would need to confirm that convention (call 2).
- B03-P01: Nothing that ingests a rent roll or lease (not in tool list).
- B03-P02: Debt sizing has no stress/sensitivity mode: for a rate or NOI stress I must call it repeatedly (only single-scenario inputs in the schema).
- B03-P02: IRR takes evenly spaced periods only; no dated XIRR (schema shows cash_flows and periods_per_year only).
- B03-P03: Storage facilities are not identified as such; only an owner name hints at it (call 4).
- B03-P03: No supply-pipeline capability at all (call 1).
- B03-P03: Calculators do not check plausibility of inputs (call 6).
- B03-P04: Confidence 0.9 on an exact-name match did not drop when the matched entity was revoked and its address differed from the tax mailing address (calls 2-3).
- B03-P04: Most large office towers I looked at had no ownership link at all (call 1), so entity resolution is partial even where a parcel is loaded.
- B03-P04: calc_compare_proposals needs the right escalation enum; 'fixed_psf' is not discoverable except from errors (call 5).
- B03-P05: The registry can search owner names, but tenant PLLCs are only found if they are registered entities in the loaded jurisdiction; the physician-group query in call 1 returned nothing.
- B03-P05: The only healthcare-relevant record I found was an owner entity, and nothing in the output says it is a medical office owner except its name.
- B03-P06: Unit labels (psf, SF) would mislead a reader of a $/kW lease output (call 4).
- B03-P06: `search` returns only FactLot documentation, which a prospect reading llms.txt might not expect (call 1).
- B03-P07: Fee-then-cap-then-gross-up ordering is left to the caller and not flagged (call 1).
- B03-P07: `fact_validate` accepts a fabricated document id and unverified quote (call 3); the word 'validated' in llms.txt and 'uncited facts are rejected' on the home page could be read to imply source checking.
- B03-P08: Insurance and property tax are one undifferentiated non-operating input (call 2).
- B03-P08: I could not test whether a hotel parcel appears in the registry at all (not tested).
- B03-P09: The registry cannot tell franchisee from franchisor because it has no tenant concept (call 1).
- B03-P09: Nothing in the product surfaces landlord repair obligations or ROFR from a lease; ingest is described as a prototype and is not in the tool list (call 4).
- B03-P10: fact_validate treats a fabricated quote, a mismatched hash, an inverted span and a dangling supersedes as valid (calls 5-6), so 'valid' does not mean 'verified' even though a partner might read it that way.
- B03-P10: No tool takes a document and a fact and re-locates the quote, which is the exact bottleneck described in my record.
- B03-P11: Cap-method choice and admin-fee handling are the caller's responsibility; the tool does not warn when readings diverge (calls 1-3).
- B03-P11: Suite-string normalisation misses embedded building prefixes (call 5).
- B03-P12: Registry misses are indistinguishable between 'not loaded' and 'not found' (calls 2-3).
- B03-P12: Citations point at the assessor extract, not an instrument (call 7).
- B03-P13: Empty registry responses carry no coverage metadata (call 4).
- B03-P13: Only one rules pack; the framework cannot yet be judged on anything I do.
- B03-P14: Stress testing requires me to script repeated calls; there is no scenario input (calls 1-4).
- B03-P14: Rate/value/NOI are independent inputs, so internal consistency across a stress is my job (call 4).
- B03-P15: Nothing here reads a lease; I would have to type the terms in, so it saves me no extraction time (all calls take hand-entered terms).
- B03-P15: Self-hosting needs Node 22.6+, which a solo firm may not have (getting-started.md).
- B03-P16: Validator accepts unknown fields despite the published schema (call 2).
- B03-P16: A fact with a contradicting quote and a human_verified method passes (call 7).
- B03-P16: 'Valid' means shape-valid only (calls 1, 2 and 7).
- B03-P17: Use-code and property-category values are returned without a legend (call 1).
- B03-P17: Ownership links are exact-name inferences at a flat 0.9 (calls 2-3); the confidence does not vary by case.
- B04-P01: compare_proposals ranks backfill and renewal without pricing downtime: two identical leases starting six months apart tie at 7.8058 (call 6); the warning says so but I still have to model downtime elsewhere
- B04-P01: Substring address search returns DC 'Ontario Pl NW' for the query 'Ontario' (call 8), a trap for an agent that does not check the state
- B04-P01: No tool for sensitivity grids or a rent-roll-based DCF, which was my first wish
- B04-P02: Denominator sensitivity: 4,200/410,000 vs 4,200/480,000 changes the balance due by about $4,579 and removes the cap reduction (call 4); the tool cannot tell me which is right
- B04-P02: No bounding box in the citation schema (calls 6)
- B04-P02: Gross-up is applied only to lines marked variable: correct, but the agent must classify every line, and the tool gives no warning if a line is misclassified
- B04-P03: Sizing uses one fixed rate and no underwriting floor, so the output is a generic number, not what an agency would size (calls 1-2)
- B04-P03: Prepayment tool cannot represent a floating-rate bridge loan with a rate cap (call 3)
- B04-P03: The hold-vs-sell comparison itself is not a tool: I had to assemble flows by hand and the tool only does the IRR/NPV arithmetic
- B04-P04: Mid-year move-in: scaling pro_rata_share by 184/365 gives 2,851.82, which overstates my annualize-then-prorate figure of 2,558.00 by 293.82 because the annual cap is not prorated, and no warning was returned (call 3). This is the exact class of error I want to avoid
- B04-P04: The tool rounds each component to cents, so a base-year result is 799.99 where the unrounded math is 800.00 (call 1)
- B04-P04: Rent schedule months run from the commencement day (15th) with no partial-month proration (call 4)
- B04-P05: Substring name search returns unrelated entities for 'MEDICAL' and nothing for an abbreviated name (calls 1, 5)
- B04-P05: The only relationship type I saw is 'owns'; there is no parent/subsidiary edge, so exposure rollup by parent cannot be done
- B04-P05: Ownership match is name-based (exact normalized name, 0.9); it is disclosed, but a 'Holdings' vs 'Properties' pair would not link
- B04-P06: No 'remaining as of date' output: I had to sum the monthly table myself (call 1)
- B04-P06: The schedule diff against Yardi is my subtraction, not a tool; the 'Yardi' schedule in call 2 is my own illustration, not real Yardi data
- B04-P06: There is no ID tool for leases or buildings, only id_parse validation (call 7); fl:space needs a building URN I cannot mint here
- B04-P07: id_parcel keeps zero padding as typed: '3-1234-56' produced a different URN from the padded BBL (call 3), so unpadded BBLs from older records will not join
- B04-P07: Registry lookups for a NYC BBL returned null and an address search returned empty (calls 4-5); only DC is in this build
- B04-P07: The NPV is arithmetic on my own penalty guess: FactLot offers no LL97 projection
- B04-P08: '0100' vs '100' and '3rd Floor' vs 'Third Floor' produce different IDs (calls 3-6), which is exactly the seller-vs-ours numbering mismatch
- B04-P08: 'Bldg 3, Unit 12' becomes the key bldg3unit12; no building/suite hierarchy is derived (call 7)
- B04-P08: Both facts passed fact_validate; whether it checks values against documents was not tested
- B04-P09: Operator brands appear as single-asset LLC names (call 2), so brand-level supply cannot be aggregated
- B04-P09: Building predicates such as building.rentable_area_sf exist as names but the parcel record I fetched carried none (calls 4, 6)
- B04-P09: Empty list for an Atlanta query looks like 'no storage there' rather than 'not covered' (call 7)
- B04-P10: The schedule rows do not foot to the headline totals by a few cents (sum of row interest 806,145.23 vs 806,145.18; sum of lease cost 4,762,480.20 vs 4,762,480.00; 19 rows off by a cent), with no stated rounding rule (call 1): precisely the audit-comment risk I described
- B04-P10: Citations do not flow through: the calculator takes plain period/amount pairs, so I must carry the amendment page references myself (call 6)
- B04-P10: The payments array must be built by me period by period; abatement tiers need manual expansion
- B04-P11: The calculator takes totals, not operator line items; the normalization step is where my time goes and it is not covered
- B04-P11: No budget-vs-actual or multi-hotel comparison output: the NOI variance (+186,550, +21.7%) is my subtraction (call 2)
- B04-P11: Search for benchmark or performance-test tools found none (call 5)
- B04-P12: The reset logic (6% of land value with floor and cap) is my arithmetic outside the tool; nothing in the catalog models a ground-rent reset (call 2)
- B04-P12: Parcel records carry assessor land area and market value, which is not a highest-and-best-use value as defined in my lease
- B04-P12: Search for zoning or land comps returned only calculators (call 7)
- B04-P13: Unit relabel workaround: output fields are named per SF (call 1), and a person reading them without context would be misled
- B04-P13: compare_proposals prefers the cheaper site per kW with no notion of power availability or leverage (call 2)
- B04-P13: No search hit for power or utility tools (call 4)
- B04-P14: A fact with no quote and no page was accepted (issues empty), so 'cited' can mean a bare document ID (call 3)
- B04-P14: The use-conflict question (my single wish) has no tool: search returned only calculators (call 7)
- B04-P14: Nothing ties a recorded REA to a parcel in the registry model I saw
- B04-P15: Additional costs are held flat for the term, so NNN expense growth is not captured (call 1 note)
- B04-P15: The ranking favours the 3-year listing because of its annualized basis and free months; the tool does not warn that terms differ
- B04-P15: Nothing supplies the inputs: asking rents, opex estimates, availability (call 5)
- B04-P16: The standard list has lease.commencement_date but no rent-commencement predicate, which is the commencement vs rent commencement ambiguity I map across 40 schemas (call 1)
- B04-P16: fact_validate accepted document_id 'fl:document:sha256-notahash' (call 6)
- B04-P16: Validation errors come back with HTTP 200 and valid: false, so a client that checks only the status code would miss them (calls 3-5)
- B04-P17: The sizing and NPV inputs (value, NOI, sale price, carry) are my guesses; the tool does arithmetic on guesses, which is what my model already has
- B04-P17: No extension or hand-back scenario tool: hold-vs-hand-back is a single NPV I assembled (call 3)
- B04-P17: Dallas address search was empty (call 5)
- B05-P01: search returns unrelated tool docs for off-topic queries instead of saying nothing matched (calls 1 and 2).
- B05-P01: Out-of-region registry queries return an empty list with no message (call 3).
- B05-P01: fact_validate accepts a source that is only a document_id, so it cannot enforce the page-and-region citation this persona requires (call 7).
- B05-P02: calc_cam_reconciliation returns provenance.sources: [] so a step cannot be tied to a clause (call 1).
- B05-P02: fact_validate passes a citation with no page and no quote (call 10), so it cannot back 'every input traceable to a page in an executed document'.
- B05-P02: One tenant per call, no stored versioned inputs (calls 1 to 4).
- B05-P03: No admin-fee or minimum-charge input appeared in the schema I read; a strip-center lease commonly has them (schema list from /v1/tools; I did not test a lease with an admin fee).
- B05-P03: Search never says 'no match' (call 3).
- B05-P03: No tool covers her top two tasks (call 3).
- B05-P04: fact_validate cannot check that a quote is in the cited document (call 5), so 'cites each obligation precisely' relies on a separate extractor.
- B05-P04: Registry results for unloaded counties are silent nulls (call 2).
- B05-P05: The comps I ranked were invented; FactLot cannot supply real ones (call 2).
- B05-P05: The discounting convention is only in the step formulas (call 1).
- B05-P06: No rules for his job; this is a gap the materials state openly (call 1, call 2).
- B05-P06: Search lists unrelated tools for a query it cannot answer (call 1).
- B05-P07: Land versus improvement areas imply floor-area ratios up to 92.7 on some parcels with no sanity flag (call 3); for BEPS an unflagged wrong GFA is a risk.
- B05-P07: Only one candidate value per field (calls 1 and 2), so the persona's 'conflicting values shown, not silently picked' cannot happen with a single source.
- B05-P08: Registry silently returns nothing for Florida (call 3).
- B05-P08: Vocabulary lists SOV-type predicates the data layer does not populate (calls 2 and 4); a reader could assume otherwise.
- B05-P09: valid_to earlier than valid_from passes validation (call 3).
- B05-P09: No calculator for this domain (call 5).
- B05-P10: The vocabulary is building-level (call 4), which is a named dealbreaker ('building-level-only specs').
- B05-P10: There is no buildout cost input as such; the user must net allowance and overage (call 1).
- B05-P11: No capex plan or reserve schedule tool; escalation requires repurposing calc_rent_schedule (call 3).
- B05-P11: IRR is annual only by the shape of the input; a monthly variant was not tested.
- B05-P12: Some land areas look implausible against improvement areas and carry no flag (call 6).
- B05-P12: Owner links are inferred from exact-name matches at confidence 0.9 (call 4), so owners whose name does not match a registered entity may have no link; I did not test a non-matching owner in this persona's calls.
- B05-P13: No tool for the job (calls 1 and 2).
- B05-P13: Search returns unrelated tool docs rather than 'no match' (calls 1 and 2).
- B05-P14: human_verified accepted without a verifier (call 2).
- B05-P14: Evidence reference can be an arbitrary string rather than a content-addressed document (call 5).
- B05-P15: No attribute search; a screen of 200 parcels required 204 calls (call 1).
- B05-P15: Some parcels have no owner fact (1600 M St NW) and some owners have no entity link (calls 1 and 2).
- B05-P16: The workaround uses debt_sizing for a loan constant and does not model recovery within the remaining lease term or the unamortized balance (calls 1 and 2).
- B05-P16: There is no tie from the amortized amount to the lease clause (call 2).
- B05-P17: Using NPV as an adder is a hack and gives no line labels (call 1).
- B06-P01: calc_walt returns only total weight and WALT, no per-lease contribution lines, and no 'share of rent expiring by year', so the 2027 rollover question is not answered (calc_walt call)
- B06-P01: There is no tool that takes an existing loan balance and returns its DSCR or debt yield; the closest is sizing, which solves for a loan (calc_debt_sizing inputs)
- B06-P01: fact_validate accepted a bbox key that the schema does not define and schema_get shows no bbox, so the 'page/bbox citation' I would want is only page plus quote (fact_validate and schema_get calls)
- B06-P02: owned_by for a 27-parcel LLC returned 24 separate condo-unit parcels at one address with no building roll-up, so a sponsor's 'properties' would be overcounted (registry_owned_by call)
- B06-P02: Cap cost has to be folded into the rate by hand; nothing checks it (calc_debt_sizing note)
- B06-P02: No tool reports loan maturities; the persona's 'affiliated properties with known loan maturities' is unmet
- B06-P03: Registry lookups for 'Dollar General', 'DG #14523' and 'Kroger' all returned empty (registry_search_entities calls)
- B06-P03: id_entity accepted a made-up 20-character string as an LEI, so I saw no check-digit validation; I did not test a real LEI (id_entity call)
- B06-P03: calc_walt counts an expired lease at 0 years inside the denominator; disclosed in a warning, but a pool WALT can differ materially if holdovers are excluded (1.4075 vs 1.5212 in my example)
- B06-P04: No Oakland or Alameda data on this server (registry_search_address)
- B06-P04: The only rules tool is the Texas deadline; no CA, OR or WA tax logic (GET /v1/tools)
- B06-P04: The inputs 1.25x DSCR and 80% LTV were mine; the tool has no agency parameter tables, so correctness of the agency inputs stays my job
- B06-P05: 'Kroger', 'Ralphs' and 'Kroger Ltd Partnership I' all returned no results; MCP 'search' only looks up FactLot's own tools, not data (registry_search_entities and search calls)
- B06-P06: Departmental expenses are one lump input, so a stress needs my own fixed/variable split (my 40/60 assumption) because the tool does not model cost behaviour (calc_hotel_metrics stress call)
- B06-P06: The tool gives NOI and DSCR but no covenant-test or annual stress workflow; each scenario is a separate call I tabulate myself
- B06-P07: No data-center operators, hyperscalers or utilities in the DC sample (registry_search_entities calls)
- B06-P07: The parcel record has no zoning, permit or power field (registry_get call)
- B06-P08: Discounted NER depends on a convention (nominal annual/12, start-of-month); I got 16.22 or 16.46 instead of 16.32 under other conventions, so a reader must be told which one (calc_net_effective_rent call)
- B06-P08: No TI/LC/downtime roll-up for a whole rent roll; each tenant is a separate call
- B06-P08: calc_walt gives no per-year expiration share, so the 2027 concentration is my own arithmetic
- B06-P09: Both Salina and Saline County searches returned empty (registry_search_address, REST and MCP)
- B06-P09: No Kansas crosswalk (registry_resolve_external returned urn null)
- B06-P10: On the landlord basis the NNN comp scores 9.84 and the gross comp 21.81 because pass-throughs are excluded and the landlord-paid expenses of the gross comp are not deducted; no warning says the two are not comparable, so an appraiser converting gross to net still has to do it by hand (calc_compare_proposals call)
- B06-P10: No field for confirming party, date or expense basis exists in the tools; only generic facts with custom predicates (schema_get call)
- B06-P11: Condo units at one address came back as 24 separate parcels with no building roll-up and no unit count (registry_search_address and registry_get calls)
- B06-P11: Search page size is capped at 100 (registry_search_address call)
- B06-P11: Building and lease URNs parse but nothing in the tool list creates them (id_parse call)
- B06-P12: The parcel record has no sale date, price, parties or document number (registry_get call)
- B06-P12: Address search misses on 'Northwest', 'N.W.', a ZIP or suite suffix (registry_search_address call)
- B06-P13: Local use codes come back as numeric strings with no labels, and $/SF within one use code in my DC sample ranged from about 128 to 1,451, so comp selection needs class/neighbourhood filters the tool does not provide (registry_get calls)
- B06-P13: The rule covers only the general deadline; tax year, county-specific practice and late relief are outside it (rule verification notes in the deadline call)
- B06-P14: A ten-year projection takes ten separate hotel_metrics calls plus npv/irr; I generated growth and cost assumptions myself, and only year one was recomputed by hand (calc_hotel_metrics calls)
- B06-P14: Only aggregate departmental expenses are accepted, so departmental flows by department are not modelled, which was my specific complaint about the LLM (calc_hotel_metrics input)
- B06-P15: I found no way to request an older formula version from the API, so reproducing last quarter's number depends on pinning a self-hosted version (formula_version and @1 tests)
- B06-P15: The roll-forward diff (what changed in NOI/value) is not a tool; I assembled it from two calls plus npv
- B06-P16: registry_get has no as-of parameter, and include_history returned the same twelve current facts with no validity dates; this sample has one snapshot so supersession could not be tested (registry_get call)
- B06-P16: The source document id cannot be fetched (fetch call)
- B06-P16: The validator did not flag a validity range that ends before it starts (fact_validate call)
- B06-P16: Aurora, Phoenix and Denver searches returned nothing on this server (registry_search_address call)
- B07-P01: Empty registry results give no 'region not loaded' signal (calls 0, 1, 3), so an agent could tell a director a parcel does not exist when the county is simply not loaded.
- B07-P01: The registry citation is dataset-level plus a record locator ('section'), not an ordinance or instrument page (call 8), so even where registry data exists it cannot meet my 'link to the source document page' rule for zoning/REA.
- B07-P01: MCP search returned an empty result for a drive-thru zoning query (call 3): there is no discovery path to say 'not covered'.
- B07-P02: Address search is brittle for exactly my use: '1000 Wisconsin Avenue Northwest' -> [] and '... Ave NW Suite 400' -> [] while '1000 Wisconsin Ave NW' resolves (calls 0-3), so double-counting of practices would persist.
- B07-P02: Two distinct parcels share the label '1001 PENNSYLVANIA AVE NW' (call 4); the search returns labels without disambiguating fields, so a building with several lots needs a follow-up call per URN.
- B07-P02: id_space needs a building URN but no tool or registry field supplies one (call 10): the parcel URN the registry returns is rejected.
- B07-P02: calc_compare_proposals output does not state its monthly timing convention (annuity-due); figures match only under it (call 11).
- B07-P03: The compare tool warns 'Proposals differ in area; $/SF/yr normalizes size but not the value of the space itself' (call 1) but gives no help to compare a 1,200 SF end cap with a 1,500 SF inline in total-cost terms beyond total_net.
- B07-P03: fact_validate only checks shape; it cannot tell me a confirmation is true or recent (calls 3, 4).
- B07-P03: Annuity-due discounting is not stated in compare_proposals output (call 1) although it is stated in the NER step text (call 2).
- B07-P04: No way to ask 'what does the registry cover' before querying; empty list vs not-loaded is ambiguous (call 3).
- B07-P04: Owner linking is by exact normalized-name match with confidence 0.9 and a stated basis (call 6), which cannot reach heirs or individuals; privacy.txt says owner names are stored only for organisations.
- B07-P04: calc_lease_vs_own is generic NPV and not a net-lease BTS model (no rent commencement/option structure).
- B07-P05: fact_validate accepted a value that contradicted its own quote (call 1), so citation integrity is not enforced by any tool I could call.
- B07-P05: Field description for weight_by does not match the enum (call 2).
- B07-P05: Entity records give a mailing address but not a lease notice address; a landlord LLC is not tied to a successor after a sale (call 5).
- B07-P06: No occupancy cost ratio, so I would still compute (rent + CAM)/sales myself.
- B07-P06: Relocation inputs (fit-out, downtime, overlap, disruption) are all caller-supplied, so the NPV is only as good as my own estimates; the tool gives no benchmark.
- B07-P07: MCP search returns titles and GitHub URLs only, not data (calls 0-3).
- B07-P07: registry_get on a DC parcel gave area/year built/use code only (call 5).
- B07-P07: IRR inputs were my own illustrative numbers.
- B07-P08: Two McDonald's legal entities with the same mailing address and no common parent or brand (calls 0-2).
- B07-P08: Brand-name searches for 'Starbucks' and 'Kroger' returned [] (calls 5, 6): legal-entity names do not reveal the brands in a center.
- B07-P09: Composition is manual: the monthly array for NPV is built by the agent, so the agent still does the arithmetic of assembling flows (calls 1, 2) -- an error there would not be caught by the tool.
- B07-P09: Outputs carry no link to a lease clause or page for the inputs; that tie-out is up to me.
- B07-P09: I rounded the monthly rate to 10 places before passing it to calc_npv (calls 1, 2); tiny differences appear versus an exact rate.
- B07-P10: All relocation costs (buildout, moving, downtime, disruption) come from me; no benchmarks (call 0).
- B07-P10: Break-even output is useful but is stated per SF per year with no sensitivity table.
- B07-P11: The amendment also contracts space, but the tool treats it as a single 'modification_extend_term'; it did not warn that a partial termination would need a proportionate ROU reduction, and returned warnings: [] (call 0).
- B07-P11: provenance.sources is empty on every computed result (call 0): inputs are not tied to source pages, so the auditor tie-out remains manual.
- B07-P11: There is no input echo field linking each payment to a document page.
- B07-P12: calc_npv will not take a single lump sum; the agent must pad with a zero (call 4).
- B07-P12: 'Estimated buyout range' and landlord appetite are not computable: nothing in the catalog.
- B07-P12: registry_owned_by returned only two parcels with 0.9 confidence for the bank in DC (call 1), unrelated to my branch footprint.

- B07-P13: additional_costs_psf_annual is flat (call 2): an FSG base-year deal with expense growth is not modeled, so NNN pass-throughs are understated (they grow) and FSG base-year exposure is not captured.
- B07-P13: Annuity-due discounting is not stated in the compare output (call 0).

- B07-P14: Profit-sharing with the landlord is not modeled.

- B07-P15: Units print as 'USD/sf/yr' for a kW comparison (call 0).
